\caption{Every clean section of the example. The inverse source half restores all intermediate raw values in reverse order. Its microscopic paths need not be reversed.}\label{smc:ct:tab:example}
\end{table}

The initial configuration is the clean $\Fq {q_0}$ controller and shuttle at $-60$, with $R$ at $0$. The exact target uses the clean $H$ controller and shuttle at $-60$, the same $R$ at $0$, and vacuum everywhere else. Its first time is
\[
2(13\,480)+192(10+5)+16=29\,856.
\]
Spatial blocking moves the left pair to $-15$, offset zero, and keeps that time. The phase construction keeps coordinate $-60$ and first reaches its phase-zero target at $119\,424$ ticks.

Here $q=6$, $J=5$, $m=6$, $d=B=5$, while the cleaned values are $q'=13$, $J'=12$, $m'=13$, $d'=10$, $B'=12$. The native generator gives $19\,416$ channels, $598\,096$ prescribed pair rows, and $1\,188\,236$ completed pair-support rows. These are example counts, not universal-machine counts.

The compact certificate has 50 core variables, 16 core squares, and 25 products. The packaged free-time version has 51 total variables and 17 squares, with time output $29\,856$. By comparison, the full cleaned exporter has 288 core variables, 37 core squares, and 144 products before the same time-output charge. The affine lift reuses the forward witness, with bridge magnitudes $N_h-1=9$ and $N_0-1=4$.

The archive's request, literal expanded certificate, unique witness, and independent checker receipt reproduce Table~\ref{smc:ct:tab:example}. They describe a small finite chain rather than a universal program.

\section{Validation and reproducibility}\label{smc:ct:sec:validation}

\subsection{What the implementations check}

The cleanup exporter validates structural freshness, builds the forward/inverse source table, composes the original polynomial, and exposes the cleaned clock. A separate checker reconstructs the wrapped program and affine outputs instead of trusting these claims in the exported JSON. It invokes the unchanged independent forward checker and validates the natural witness, optional literal polynomial expansion, and full resource ledger. Its decoded cleaned trace is compared with actual source execution and literal lattice evolution.

The producer tests exercise all primitive operations, both nonzero prime-three residues, guarded branch/merge behavior, a mixed chain with unequal original endpoint values, $h=0$, immediate and delayed stalls, a growing nonhalting prefix, raw cofactors, loader interfaces, invalid freshness, clock symmetry, and maliciously altered certificates. Successful lattice checks compare the entire target and every canonical section, test conservation and inverses, and require prescribed pair rows through the first hit. Selected cases execute all native, spatial, and phase ticks.

The independent lattice harness separately builds the wrapper, executes source instructions, recognizes the full canonical configurations, and steps the generated collision and stream maps without calling the compiler's ordinary step or ready helpers. Its certificate audit exercises valid interfaces and rejects deliberate mutations. The separate lift audit compares explicit forward/reverse variable reuse with the full cleaned exporter and its frozen independent checker.

\paragraph{Finite evidence and theorem evidence.}
Finite tests verify concrete implementations and boundary cases. The all-input restoration and nonhalting exclusion follow from the proofs, the full-space reversible behavior from the compiler, and undecidability from the classical source dependency. No finite collection of successful traces proves universality, and a growing finite prefix does not alone prove nonhalting. The archive records each test's actual scope rather than conflating these forms of evidence.

\paragraph{Replay summary.} The completed release receipt records the exact test counts and subject hashes. The independently checked lattice sample comprises 14 small sources and 35 runs, including 26 successful cleanups and nine stuck inputs. It covers 111\,712 native ticks, 9\,456 spatial ticks, and 37\,824 phase ticks. The independent certificate pass checks 61 valid model/interface cases, rejects 174 mutations and 19 malformed syntax or API cases, and enumerates 1\,536 natural assignments for prime-three zero-test branches. The producer checks 77 affine-lift cases; the independent lift audit checks 24 cases and 96 public-coordinate naming-collision regressions. The producer lattice suite covers 45 halting runs, 21 stalls, 197\,374 native ticks, 21\,120 spatial ticks, and 84\,480 phase ticks; it also checks 140 clock-symmetry cases. Normal and optimized Python runs are recorded separately.

\subsection{Portable replay and source provenance}

The portable archive includes this standalone editable LaTeX source and PDF, the cleanup code and independent checker, literal examples, the five exact frozen Python dependencies, independent audit harnesses, receipts, and build/replay scripts. Python replay uses only the standard library. PDF rebuild uses a conventional TeX installation and the packages declared at the top of the source. No third-party paper PDFs are redistributed.

All package files are covered by a SHA-256 manifest except the manifest itself. A verifier checks both hashes and the expected file set. The archive safety check rejects absolute paths, parent traversal, duplicate members, and symbolic links. Fresh replay is performed from an extracted copy rather than from the source working directory. Temporary generated outputs remain outside the sealed file set. The final PDF is rendered and every page is visually checked.

The provenance ledger records hashes for the five frozen dependencies, the exact extension sources, source-audit scripts, and the companion report that establishes inherited results. Independent harnesses are made portable only by changing dependency/output path selection; that adaptation is recorded explicitly and the adapted copies are rerun. Receipts identify which subject code was actually tested. References link to primary publications; packaging does not silently substitute a different local-rule generator.

\wtag\ \textbf{The release in this report.} These are the delivered names. Source~15's top-level programs and its \path{audit/} programs are shipped as \path{code/15-clean-targets-*}; its \path{examples/}, \path{receipts/} and \path{provenance/} records as \path{data/15-clean-targets-*}; and \path{CLEAN-TARGET-THEOREM.md}, \path{SOURCE_PROVENANCE.md} and \path{audit/INDEPENDENT-AUDIT.md} as \path{15-clean-targets-*} at the report root. ``The five exact frozen Python dependencies'' (\path{vendor/*.py}) are byte copies of source~14's programs and are shipped once, as \path{code/14-three-mass-*}. Not shipped: the manuscript, PDF and README, the SHA-256 manifest and \path{verify_manifest.py}. The programs import \path{vendor/} by its delivered name, so they must be run in a restored layout; on Windows \path{verify_manifest.py} rejects every nested member (it compares native paths with \texttt{/}-separated names), and the replay's byte-exact example comparison fails on CRLF output although the content agrees, so the replay is run without the manifest there (README).

\section{Boundaries and precise next questions}\label{smc:ct:sec:questions}

The construction settles the mass threshold for exact-pair reachability in the stated weighted model. It also settles radius one as a sufficient radius with three conserved mass units. Several stronger or more concrete claims remain distinct.
\begin{enumerate}
\item\label{smc:ct:q:universal} \textbf{A fully expanded universal instance.} Instantiate a particular universal reversible two-counter table with fresh entry and terminal halt, provide its input encoder, and freeze the resulting cleaned local rule. This would yield an exact universal alphabet and row count. The small examples in this archive do not perform that instantiation. (\wtag\ Compare source~14's Question~\ref{smc:tm:q:source} and Part~IV's first question; all three are open.)
\item\label{smc:ct:q:fixedtarget} \textbf{One target independent of the input.} Determine whether a suitable fixed reversible weighted CA can have undecidable reachability to one chosen finite configuration from a computable input family. The present restoration construction gives $y_N$, not a single $y$. Injectivity and the backward orbit of a fixed target must be handled explicitly; no claim of impossibility follows from this report.
\item\label{smc:ct:q:overhead} \textbf{Smaller finite-state overhead.} Reduce the duplicate state space, residue clock, or two bridge costs while retaining global reversibility and exact scratch cleanup. Compare improvements using the same target convention and all resource ledgers. A faster logical inverse cannot be assumed to reverse microscopic paths. (\wtag\ Compare source~14's Question~\ref{smc:tm:q:overhead}.)
\item\label{smc:ct:q:mass} \textbf{The integer-state mass convention.} Find the corresponding sharp reversible threshold when each cell state's integer value is its conserved mass. Equal-weight channel types used here cannot be transferred by relabeling. This is a different model-specific question. (\wtag\ Compare source~14's Question~\ref{smc:tm:q:mass}, answered in part by Part~\ref{smc:su:part}, as the note there says.)
\item\label{smc:ct:q:unbounded} \textbf{A costed unbounded-time certificate.} Compose this frontend with an explicit fixed-arity Diophantine encoding of unbounded computation, and state degree, variable costs, and the resulting fiber properties. The fixed-horizon affine lift alone supplies no finite-fold or single-fold unbounded-time conclusion. (\wtag\ Compare source~14's Question~\ref{smc:tm:q:unbounded}.)
\item\label{smc:ct:q:mechanized} \textbf{Mechanized compiler composition.} Formalize the separated syntax, inverse source transform, prescribed collision routes, and zero-fiber correspondence in one proof system. The key interfaces to preserve are exact absolute configurations, natural domains, and the distinction between source steps and CA ticks.
\end{enumerate}

There is close geometric prior art. Brandt, Portmann and Uitto~\cite{brandt2020} use three finite-state agents, a prime-exponent gap, and reflected-shuttle arithmetic. The companion report \textup{[source~14, Section~\ref{smc:tm:sec:limits}]} compares those mechanisms with the global reversible CA and its cleanup obligations. The extension here does not claim a literature-wide priority result or a new general principle of uncomputation. Its conclusion is the explicit exact-target strengthening, under the model and dependencies stated above.
